Requirements:\n21: \n22: - `~/.secrets/onit/host_address.txt` and `~/.secrets/onit/api_key.txt` \u2014\n23:   the same files `../another-harness/scripts/run-onit.sh` and\n24:   `investigation/probe/run_probe.sh` read. The script sources them into\n25:   its own process's environment only; the API key is never logged,\n26:   printed, or written into a config file in the clear.\n27: - A Python 3 interpreter on `PATH` able to run `python3 -m venv` \u2014 the\n28:   venv itself does **not** need to exist ahead of time. `investigation/probe/.venv`\n29:   (the same venv Task 1/3's probes used) doubles as dsh-tui's runtime venv;\n30:   if it isn't there yet, `scripts/run-dsh-tui.sh` creates it and installs\n31:   everything in [`requirements.txt`](requirements.txt) (`deepseek-harness-sdk`,\n32:   `rich`) automatically on first run. If it already exists (e.g. you ran a\n33:   probe already), the script reuses it as-is and only installs `rich` if\n34:   missing.\n35: \n36: Override the provider or model without editing any file:\n37: \n38: ```sh\n39: DSH_TUI_PROVIDER=onit DSH_TUI_MODEL=Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B scripts/run-dsh-tui.sh\n40: ```\n41: \n42: Type at the `\u27a4` prompt and press Enter to send a turn. Type `\\bye`, or\n43: press Ctrl+C / Ctrl+D, to exit.\n44: \n45: ### Scrolling the chat history\n46: \n47: While typing at the `\u27a4` prompt, these keys move the visible window over\n48: the chat history instead of being inserted into the input line:\n49: \n50: - **PgUp** or **Ctrl+U** \u2014 scroll back (older messages)\n51: - **PgDn** or **Ctrl+F** \u2014 scroll forward (newer messages)\n52: - **Home** \u2014 jump to the oldest message\n53: - **End** \u2014 jump to the newest message\n54: \n55: There's also a vim-style scroll mode: a standalone **Esc** at the prompt\n56: toggles it, then `j`/`k` (or the arrow keys) step one page,\n57: `gg`/`G` jump to the oldest/newest message, and `Enter`/`i`/`Esc` return\n58: to typing.\n59: \n60: The visible window is **height-aware**, not a fixed number of messages:\n61: `ChatUI` measures each message's real rendered height (via Rich's word\n62: wrap) against however many rows are actually free in the terminal right\n63: now, and shows however many trailing messages fit. `display_messages`\n64: (default 15, passed to `ChatUI(...)`) is a *ceiling* on that count, not\n65: the count itself \u2014 a handful of verbose responses can fill the whole\n66: window well before `display_messages` is reached, and a terminal resize\n67: changes how many messages fit on the next repaint with no extra\n68: keypress needed.\n69: \n70: The footer under the chat panel always shows the visible range and the\n71: total message count (e.g. `Showing 12-26 of 140 messages`), reflecting\n72: the actual (height-fitted) window size, not `display_messages`. Once\n73: you've scrolled back from the bottom it also says so and names the key\n74: that returns you to the latest messages (`\u2014 scrolled, End to jump to\n75: latest`).\n76: \n77: Note: because the window size varies with position (it's driven by how\n78: tall the messages at that position happen to be, not a fixed count),\n79: `PgUp`/`k` followed by `PgDn`/`j` is not guaranteed to land you back on\n80: the exact same window you started from. `End` (or `G` in scroll mode)\n81: is the reliable way back to the bottom \u2014 it always jumps to offset 0\n82: rather than retracing steps.\n83: \n84: ## Known limitations\n85: \n86: - **No live token streaming.** The answer lands complete, not\n87:   token-by-token \u2014 `assistant/message` arrives as one whole-string event\n88:   on this backend (confirmed up to ~1.2k chars, investigation/NOTES.md\n89:   \"Probe 3\" finding (b)), so `ChatUI.stream_token()` is called once per\n90:   turn with the full text. A spinner runs while the turn is in flight so\n91:   the loop never looks hung, but it is not a typewriter effect.\n92: - **Ctrl+C mid-turn exits the client and may leave the run orphaned\n93:   server-side.** `ChatUI.get_user_input()` raises on Ctrl+C/EOF and the\n94:   main loop exits cleanly, but whether dsh's SDK supports cancelling an\n95:   in-flight `session_prompt` is unconfirmed (investigation/NOTES.md\n96:   \"Remaining unknowns\"). Ctrl+C at the `\u27a4` prompt (between turns) is\n97:   always clean; Ctrl+C during a turn is not guaranteed to stop the\n98:   work happening on the server.\n99: - **Single session, no resume across restarts.** `harness.start_session(id)`\n100:   is called once per process and a fresh session id is generated per run;\n101:   closing and reopening the client starts a new conversation with no\n102:   history from the previous run.\n103: - **Scrolling is message-granular, not line-granular.** The scrollback\n104:   window (`ChatUI._visible_messages()`) now consults the real terminal\n105:   size and each message's actual rendered height (`_content_budget()`,\n106:   `_message_height()`), so it fits as many trailing messages as the\n107:   terminal has room for \u2014 but it still fits or drops whole messages, not\n108:   lines within one. A single message taller than the whole terminal\n109:   still overflows the visible area; sub-message (line-granular)\n110:   scrolling is not implemented.\n111: - **No live real-terminal verification of the height-aware scroll\n112:   window.** The behavior above is covered by an extensive unit and pty\n113:   test suite (measured/pinned terminal dimensions, real key-dispatch\n114:   loop), but no agent that built or reviewed it had an interactive tty\n115:   to confirm against. If you hit anything that looks off with a real\n116:   verbose session (`show_logs=True`, a few long turns, Esc, `k`/`j`),\n117:   it's worth a spot-check before relying on it further.\n118: - **Chat history is capped at `max_messages` (1000).** Raised in\n119:   `dsh_tui/cli.py` from `ChatUI`'s original default of 100 specifically\n120:   so the new scroll feature has meaningful depth to reach \u2014 the old\n121:   default would have capped scrollback at roughly 25 tool-using turns\n122:   (each turn appends 2+ messages with `show_logs=True`).\n123: \n124: ## Growth table \u2014 capability-driven, not calendar-driven\n125: \n126: Copied verbatim from `PLAN.md` (\"Growth table \u2014 capability-driven, not\n127: calendar-driven\") so a future session enables a row instead of\n128: re-deriving it. Every row is one more method call on a class already in\n129: `dsh_tui/chat_ui.py`, except the two marked \u26a0, which need an external\n130: answer first and are called out honestly rather than implied to be free.\n131: \n132: | You hit this | Do this | Read |\n133: |---|---|---|\n134: | The answer landing all at once feels dead | Throttle the single `stream_token` into timed slices in the driver's `assistant/message` handler (~5 lines). Label it in code and UI as an approximation, never as streaming | `chat_ui.py:891` `stream_token` |\n135: | You want the model's reasoning visible | \u26a0 Needs Task 1 finding (c) first \u2014 a separate reasoning event, or `<think>`-wrapped text the existing `_filter_display_token` already strips.
